gsm_gen_slot_acc_add = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { which = gsm_t_as value <= 100 } }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { which = gsm_t_as value <= 50 } }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { which = gsm_t_as value <= 25 } }
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { which = gsm_t_as value <= 13 } }
					if = {
						limit = { check_variable = { which = gsm_t_as value <= 7 } }
						if = {
							limit = { check_variable = { which = gsm_t_as value <= 4 } }
							if = {
								limit = { check_variable = { which = gsm_t_as value <= 2 } }
								if = {
									limit = { check_variable = { which = gsm_t_as value <= 1 } }
									if = {
										limit = { check_variable = { which = gsm_t_as value = 1 } }
										event_target:gsm_exchange = {
											change_variable = { which = gsm_s1_rev value = prev.gsm_t_arev }
											change_variable = { which = gsm_s1_inp value = prev.gsm_t_ainp }
											change_variable = { which = gsm_s1_opx value = prev.gsm_t_aopx }
											change_variable = { which = gsm_s1_dep value = prev.gsm_t_adep }

											change_variable = { which = gsm_s1_fu value = prev.gsm_t_afu }

											change_variable = { which = gsm_s1_fpr value = prev.gsm_t_afpr }
											change_variable = { which = gsm_s1_fk value = prev.gsm_t_afk }
											change_variable = { which = gsm_s1_fn value = prev.gsm_t_afor }

gsm_gen_slot_acc_take = {
	set_variable = { which = gsm_rev value = 0 }
	set_variable = { which = gsm_inp value = 0 }
	set_variable = { which = gsm_opx value = 0 }
	set_variable = { which = gsm_dep value = 0 }
	set_variable = { which = gsm_k value = 0 }
	set_variable = { which = gsm_t_uk value = 0 }
	set_variable = { which = gsm_n_assets value = 0 }
	set_variable = { which = gsm_jobs value = 0 }
	set_variable = { which = gsm_jobs_f value = 0 }
	set_variable = { which = gsm_ftax value = 0 }
	set_variable = { which = gsm_fpr value = 0 }
	set_variable = { which = gsm_kf value = 0 }
	set_variable = { which = gsm_n_fa value = 0 }
	set_variable = { which = gsm_t_rc value = 0 }
	set_variable = { which = gsm_sub value = 0 }
	set_variable = { which = gsm_hcl_m value = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { which = gsm_slot value <= 100 } }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { which = gsm_slot value <= 50 } }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { which = gsm_slot value <= 25 } }
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { which = gsm_slot value <= 13 } }
					if = {
						limit = { check_variable = { which = gsm_slot value <= 7 } }
						if = {
							limit = { check_variable = { which = gsm_slot value <= 4 } }
							if = {
								limit = { check_variable = { which = gsm_slot value <= 2 } }
								if = {
									limit = { check_variable = { which = gsm_slot value <= 1 } }
									if = {
										limit = { check_variable = { which = gsm_slot value = 1 } }

										set_variable = { which = gsm_opx value = event_target:gsm_exchange.gsm_s1_opx }

										set_variable = { which = gsm_k value = event_target:gsm_exchange.gsm_s1_k }
